types = {
	steal_small_arms_INDO_COMMIE = {
		category = insurgency_raids_state
 
		days_to_prepare = 14

		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
		}

		command_power = 1

		allowed = {
			OR = {
				tag = VIN
				tag = VIE
				tag = LOS
				tag = NLF
				tag = MEO
				tag = NUN
				tag = FUL
				tag = TAI
				tag = TAM
				tag = THO
				tag = PQC
			}
		}
		visible = {
			is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = ROOT.id }
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 8
			}
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
		}
		available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
			#Immediate Does Not Require War
		}

		show_target = {
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					tag = FRE
				}
				FROM = {
					tag = FRA
				}
				FROM = {
					tag = CCC
				}
				FROM = {
					tag = VIE
				}
			}

		}

		arrow = {
		    type = line
		}

		essential_equipment = {
			#infantry_equipment = 50
		}
		additional_equipment = {
			# list of equipment archetypes and numbers
		}
		
		target_type = {
			building = {
				type = arms_factory
			}
		}
		
		unit_requirements = {
            battalion_types = { infantry = { min = 2 } }
		}
		unit_requirements = {
            battalion_types = { militia = { min = 2 } }
		}
        unit_requirements = { 
            battalion_types = { marine = { min = 2 } } 
        }
        unit_requirements = { 
            battalion_types = { mechanized_marine = { min = 2 } } 
        }
        unit_requirements = { 
            battalion_types = { mountaineers = { min = 2 } } 
        }
        unit_requirements = { 
            battalion_types = { airborne = { min = 2 } } 
        }
        unit_requirements = { 
            battalion_types = { mechanized_airborne = { min = 2 } } 
        }
        unit_requirements = { 
            battalion_types = { motorized_infantry = { min = 2 } } 
        }
        unit_requirements = { 
            battalion_types = { mechanized_infantry = { min = 2 } } 
        }
        unit_requirements = { 
            battalion_types = { armored_infantry = { min = 2 } } 
        }
        unit_requirements = { 
            battalion_types = { ffl = { min = 2 } } 
        }
        unit_requirements = { 
            battalion_types = { air_cavalry = { min = 2 } } 
        }
		starting_point = {
		    types = { air_base naval_base }
		}

		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					# Damage to unit
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 30
						str_damage = 10
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 1
						tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
					
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaTotalAntiCommunistScore = 1
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 1
						tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
					}
				}
				# Other unit effects
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 10
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					# Damage to unit
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 5
					}
					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = infantry_equipment
							amount = 100
							producer = FRA
						}
					}

					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 1
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 1
						tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
				}
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 100
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					# Damage to unit
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 25
						str_damage = 2
					}
					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = infantry_equipment
							amount = 200
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 2
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 2
						tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.2
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
				}
				# Other unit effects
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 250
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					# Damage to unit
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 0
					}

					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = infantry_equipment
							amount = 500
							producer = FRA
						}
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = support_equipment
							amount = 100
							producer = FRA
						}
					}

					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 5
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 5
						tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
				}
				# Other unit effects
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 500
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
					}
			}
		}

		success_factors = {
			disaster = {
				base = 0.05
			}
			success = {
				base = 0.4

				# Raiding unit modifiers
				experience = {
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 0.5
				}

				organisation = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 100
				}
				strength = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 1
				}
				recon = {
					weight = 0.08
					reference = 10
				}

				### Target Modifiers
				resistance = {
					reference = 100
					start_reference = 20 # Resistance below 20 doesn't help
					weight = 0.4
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.25
					start_weight = -0.25
				}


				anti_air = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 0
					weight = 0
				}
				enemy_units = {
					reference = 2 # More than 2 DIVs in the target Province shouldn't make any difference
					weight = -0.5
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.2
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}

			critical = {
				base = 0.15
			}
		}
		
	}

				# Other unit effects
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 250
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = {
				}
				actor_effects = {
					# Damage to unit
					raid_damage_units = {
						org_damage = 20
						str_damage = 0
					}

					var:actor_country = {
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = infantry_equipment
							amount = 500
							producer = FRA
						}
						add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
							type = support_equipment
							amount = 100
							producer = FRA
						}
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 5
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintangScore = 5
						tooltip = Add_to_Kuomintang_Victory_Points_TT
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = -5
						tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
					}
					# Unit Experience
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
				}
				# Other unit effects
				division_effects = {
					add_divisional_commander_xp = 500
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_paradrop_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
					}
			}
		}

			disaster = {
				base = 0.05
			}
			success = {
				base = 0.4

				# Raiding unit modifiers
				experience = {
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 0.5
				}

				organisation = {
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
					reference = 100
				}

					start_weight = -0.25

	raid_supply_node_commie = {
		category = insurgency_raids_state

		}

		command_power = 1

		allowed = {

		}
		visible = {

			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }

		available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
			#Immediate Does Not Require War
		}

		show_target = {
			OR = {
				FROM = {

				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = FROM.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = FROM.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id }
				}
			}

			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}

			type = line
		}

					}

						}

	light_bombing_raid_commie = {
		days_to_prepare = 20

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
		}

		category = insurgency_raids

		command_power = 5

		allowed = {
		}

		# Raid Type is visible
		visible = {
			is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = ROOT.id }
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
		}
		# Specific Target is visible
		show_target = {
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = FROM.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = FROM.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = FROM.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id }
				}
			}

			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}

		# Can prepare
		available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}

		target_type = {
            province = any

		}
		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon

		arrow = {
		    type = air
		}
        #unit_model = {
        #    type = equipment
        #}

		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = { air_transport }
				amount = { min = 24 }
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = { cas }
				amount = { min = 24 }
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = { fighter }
				amount = { min = 24 }
			}
		}

		starting_point = {
		    types = { air_base }
		}

		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.65

				# Raiding unit modifiers
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1.0
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100.0 # As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1 # At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1 # The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1
				}

				### Target Modifiers
				anti_air = {
					reference = 5
					weight = -0.25
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 500 # More than 500 fighters won't really make any difference
					weight = -0.2
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.3
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}

			critical = {
				base = 0.15
			}
		}

		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
                        damage_units = {
                            province = var:ROOT.target_province
                            damage = 0.05
                            org_damage = 0.05
                            str_damage = 0.05
                            ratio = yes
                        }
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.4
                        ratio = yes
                    }
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = victim_country }
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 5
							tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintangScore = 5
							tooltip = Add_to_Kuomintang_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = victim_country }
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 5
							tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 5
							tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = victim_country }
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 5
							tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 5
							tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = victim_country }
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 5
							tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = 5
							tooltip = Add_to_Ethnic_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
					}
			}
			limited_success = {
			    victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
                        damage_units = {
                            province = var:ROOT.target_province
                            damage = 0.1
                            org_damage = 0.1
                            str_damage = 0.1
                            ratio = yes
                        }
					}

			    }
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.05
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 5
						tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 5
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
                        damage_units = {
                            province = var:ROOT.target_province
                            damage = 0.2
                            org_damage = 0.2
                            str_damage = 0.2
                            ratio = yes
                        }
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.2
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 10
						tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 10
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
			    victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
                        damage_units = {
                            province = var:ROOT.target_province
                            damage = 0.3
                            org_damage = 0.3
                            str_damage = 0.3
                            ratio = yes
                        }
					}
			    }
				actor_effects = {
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
					add_to_variable = {
						FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 25
						tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
					}
					add_to_variable = {
						global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 25
						tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
					}
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Tactical Bombing Raid Level 4 (Normal Bombing)
	tactical_bombing_raid_anti_commie = {
		days_to_prepare = 20

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
		}

		category = insurgency_raids

		command_power = 5

		allowed = {
		}

		# Raid Type is visible
		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id }
				is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id }
				is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id }
			}
			check_variable = {
				global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
			}
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
		}
		# Specific Target is visible
		show_target = {
			OR = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id }
				}
			}

			var:target_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = VIN
					is_core_of = CAM
					is_core_of = LOS
				}
			}
		}

		# Can prepare
		available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
		}
		launchable = {
		}

		target_type = {
            province = any

		}
		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon

		arrow = {
		    type = air
		}
        #unit_model = {
        #    type = equipment
        #}

		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = { tactical_bomber }
				amount = { min = 24 }
			}
		}

		starting_point = {
		    types = { air_base }
		}

		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.65

				# Raiding unit modifiers
				experience = {
					weight = 0.2
					start_weight = -0.2
					reference = 1.0
				}
				air_agility = {
					reference = 100.0 # As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
					weight = 0.1 # At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
					start_weight = -0.1 # The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
				}
				reliability = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.1
				}
				air_defence = {
					reference = 100
					weight = 0.05
					start_weight = -0.05
				}
				air_superiority = {
					reference = 1
					weight = 0.1
					start_weight = -0.1
				}

				### Target Modifiers
				anti_air = {
					reference = 5
					weight = -0.25
				}
				interception = {
					reference = 500 # More than 500 fighters won't really make any difference
					weight = -0.2
				}
				intel = {
					weight = 0.3
					#start_reference = 50
					reference = 100
				}
			}

			critical = {
				base = 0.15
			}
		}

		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
                        damage_units = {
                            province = var:ROOT.target_province
                            damage = 0.05
                            org_damage = 0.05
                            str_damage = 0.05
                            ratio = yes
                        }
					}
				}
				actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.4
                        ratio = yes
                    }
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = victim_country }
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 10
							tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 10
							tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = victim_country }
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 10
							tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
						}
						add_to_variable = {
							FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintangScore = 10
							tooltip = Add_to_Kuomintang_Victory_Points_TT
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id }
							}
							add_to_variable = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = -10
								tooltip = Add_to_Ethnic_Victory_Points_TT
							}
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = {
								is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id }
							}
							add_to_variable = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = -10
								tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
							}
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = {
								is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id }
							}
							add_to_variable = {
								FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = -10
								tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
							}
						}
					}
					else = { }
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "raid_failed_entity"
					animation = "attack"
					}
			}
			limited_success = {
			    victim_effects = {
					var:target_state = {
                        damage_units = {
                            province = var:ROOT.target_province
                            damage = 0.15
                            org_damage = 0.15
                            str_damage = 0.15
                            ratio = yes
                        }
					}

			    }
				actor_effects = {
					raid_damage_units = {
						plane_loss = 0.05
						ratio = yes
					}
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = victim_country }
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 10
                            tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
                        }
                        if = {

                            add_to_variable = {

                            }

                            }
                            add_to_variable = {
                                FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 10
                                tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
                            }
                        }
                        else_if = {
                            limit = {
                                is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id }
                            }
                            add_to_variable = {
                                FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 10
                                tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    else_if = {
                        limit = {
                            is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = victim_country }
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 10
                            tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintangScore = 10

                        }
                        if = {

                                is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id }
                            }
                            add_to_variable = {
                                FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnicScore = 10
                                tooltip = Add_to_Ethnic_Victory_Points_TT
                            }
                        }
                        else_if = {

                            }
                            add_to_variable = {
                                FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 10
                                tooltip = Add_to_French_Victory_Points_TT
                            }
                        }

    barrel_bombing_raid_commie = {
        days_to_prepare = 20

        ai_will_do = {
            base = 2
        }

        category = insurgency_raids

        command_power = 5

        allowed = {
        }

        # Raid Type is visible
        visible = {
            is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = ROOT.id }
            check_variable = {
                global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
            }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        # Specific Target is visible
        show_target = {
            OR = {
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id }
                }
            }

            var:target_state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }

        # Can prepare
        available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
        }
        launchable = {
        }

        target_type = {
            state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }
        custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid

            type = air

				type = { cas }
				amount = { min = 24 }
			}
		}
		unit_requirements = {
			equipment = {
				type = { fighter }
				amount = { min = 24 }
			}
		}

		starting_point = {
		    types = { air_base }
		}

        success_factors = {
            success = {
                base = 0.5

                # Raiding unit modifiers
                experience = {
                    weight = 0.2
                    start_weight = -0.2
                    reference = 1.0
                }
                air_agility = {
                    reference = 100.0 # As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
                    weight = 0.1 # At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
                    start_weight = -0.1 # The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
                }
                reliability = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }
                air_defence = {
                    reference = 100
                    weight = 0.05

                }

                    reference = 1

                }

                anti_air = {
                    reference = 5
                    weight = -0.25
                }
                interception = {
                    reference = 500 # More than 500 fighters won't really make any difference
                    weight = -0.2
                }

                    weight = 0.3

                    reference = 100

    civilian_bombing_raid_commie = {
        days_to_prepare = 20

        ai_will_do = {
            base = 2
        }

        category = insurgency_raids

        command_power = 5

        allowed = {
        }

        # Raid Type is visible
        visible = {
            is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = ROOT.id }
            check_variable = {
                global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
            }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        # Specific Target is visible
        show_target = {
            OR = {
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id }
                }
            }

            var:target_state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }

        # Can prepare
        available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
        }
        launchable = {
        }

        target_type = {
            state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }
        custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
        target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon

        arrow = {
            type = air
        }
        #unit_model = {
        #    type = equipment
        #}

        unit_requirements = {
            equipment = {
                type = { tactical_bomber }
                amount = { min = 24 }
            }
        }

        starting_point = {
            types = { air_base }
        }

        success_factors = {
            success = {
                base = 0.65

                # Raiding unit modifiers
                experience = {
                    weight = 0.2
                    start_weight = -0.2
                    reference = 1.0
                }
                air_agility = {
                    reference = 100.0 # As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
                    weight = 0.1 # At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
                    start_weight = -0.1 # The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
                }
                reliability = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }
                air_defence = {
                    reference = 100
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.05
                }
                air_superiority = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.1
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }

                ### Target Modifiers
                anti_air = {
                    reference = 5
                    weight = -0.25
                }
                interception = {
                    reference = 500 # More than 500 fighters won't really make any difference
                    weight = -0.2
                }
                intel = {
                    weight = 0.3
                    #start_reference = 50
                    reference = 100
                }
            }

            critical = {
                base = 0.15
            }
        }

        success_levels = {
            failure = {
                victim_effects = {
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.4
                        ratio = yes
                    }
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = victim_country }
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 10
                            tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintangScore = 10
                            tooltip = Add_to_Kuomintang_Victory_Points_TT
                        }
                    }
                    else_if = {
                        limit = {
                            is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = victim_country }
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 10
                            tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependenceScore = 10
                            tooltip = Add_to_Independent_Victory_Points_TT
                        }
                    }
                    else_if = {
                        limit = {
                            is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = victim_country }
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_B_Points = 10
                            tooltip = Add_to_De_Escalation_Phase_TT
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFranceScore = 10

    nerve_weapon_raid_anti_commie = {
        days_to_prepare = 20

        ai_will_do = {
            base = 2
        }

        category = insurgency_raids

        command_power = 5

        allowed = {
        }

        # Raid Type is visible
        visible = {
            has_tech = nbc_nerve_weapon_0
            OR = {
                is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id }
                is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id }
                is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id }
            }
            check_variable = {
                global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
            }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        # Specific Target is visible
        show_target = {
            OR = {
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id }
                }
            }

            var:target_state = {
                has_border_war = yes
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }

        # Can prepare
        available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
        }
        launchable = {
        }

        target_type = {
            state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }
        custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
        target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon

        arrow = {
            type = air
        }

        #    type = equipment
        #}

            equipment = {
                type = { tactical_bomber }
                amount = { min = 24 }
            }
        }

        starting_point = {
            types = { air_base }
        }

        success_factors = {
            success = {
                base = 0.65

                # Raiding unit modifiers
                experience = {
                    weight = 0.2
                    start_weight = -0.2
                    reference = 1.0
                }
                air_agility = {
                    reference = 100.0 # As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
                    weight = 0.1 # At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
                    start_weight = -0.1 # The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
                }
                reliability = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }
                air_defence = {
                    reference = 100
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.05
                }
                air_superiority = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.1
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }

                ### Target Modifiers
                anti_air = {
                    reference = 5
                    weight = -0.25
                }

                    reference = 500 #
                    weight = -0.2
                }
                intel = {
                    weight = 0.3
                    #start_reference = 50
                    reference = 100
                }
            }

            critical = {
                base = 0.15
            }
        }

        success_levels = {
            failure = {
                victim_effects = {
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.4
                        ratio = yes
                    }
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
                }

                    entity = "raid_failed_entity"
                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }
            limited_success = {

                                org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                ratio = yes
                            }
                            set_border_war_data = {
                                attacker = Target_State
                                defender = defender_state_test
                                defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                            }
                        }
                        else = {
                            set_temp_variable = {
                                defender_state_test = THIS
                            }
                            every_neighbor_state = {
                                limit = {
                                    set_temp_variable = {
                                        attacker_state_test = THIS
                                    }
                                    has_border_war_between = {
                                        attacker = attacker_state_test
                                        defender = defender_state_test
                                    }
                                }
                                set_temp_variable = {
                                    Target_State = THIS
                                }
                                damage_units = {
                                    state = target_state
                                    damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    ratio = yes
                                }
                                set_border_war_data = {
                                    attacker = Target_State
                                    defender = defender_state_test
                                    attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            civilian_casualties = state_population
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.006
                        }
                        multiply_variable = {
                            civilian_casualties = ROOT.Civilian_Modifier
                        }
                        add_manpower = civilian_casualties
                    }
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
                    add_to_variable = {
                        global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 50
                        tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
                    }
                }
                visual_effect = {
                    entity = "air_raids_1_critical_success_entity"
                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }
        }

    nerve_weapon_raid_commie = {
        days_to_prepare = 20

        ai_will_do = {
            base = 2
        }

        category = insurgency_raids

        command_power = 5

        allowed = {
        }

        # Raid Type is visible
        visible = {
            has_tech = nbc_nerve_weapon_0
            is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = ROOT.id }
            check_variable = {
                global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
            }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        # Specific Target is visible
        show_target = {
            OR = {
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id }
                }
            }

            var:target_state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }

        # Can prepare
        available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
        }
        launchable = {
        }

        target_type = {
            state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }
        custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
        target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon

        arrow = {
            type = air
        }
        #unit_model = {
        #    type = equipment
        #}

        unit_requirements = {
            equipment = {
                type = { tactical_bomber }
                amount = { min = 24 }
            }
        }

        starting_point = {
            types = { air_base }
        }

        success_factors = {
            success = {
                base = 0.65

                # Raiding unit modifiers
                experience = {
                    weight = 0.2
                    start_weight = -0.2
                    reference = 1.0
                }
                air_agility = {
                    reference = 100.0 # As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
                    weight = 0.1 # At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
                    start_weight = -0.1 # The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
                }
                reliability = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }
                air_defence = {
                    reference = 100
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.05
                }
                air_superiority = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.1
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }

                ### Target Modifiers
                anti_air = {
                    reference = 5
                    weight = -0.25
                }
                interception = {
                    reference = 500 #
                    weight = -0.2
                }
                intel = {
                    weight = 0.3
                    #start_reference = 50
                    reference = 100
                }
            }

            critical = {
                base = 0.15
            }
        }

        success_levels = {
            failure = {
                victim_effects = {
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.4
                        ratio = yes
                    }
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
                }
                visual_effect = {
                    entity = "raid_failed_entity"
                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }
            limited_success = {
                victim_effects = {
                    var:target_state = {
                        ROOT = {
                            if = {
                                limit = {
                                    has_tech = nbc_nerve_weapon_5
                                }
                                set_variable = { Chemical_Damage = 0.50 }
                                set_variable = { ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.008 }
                                PREV = { add_province_modifier = { static_modifiers = { A_234 } days = 60 province = { all_provinces = yes } } }
                            }
                            else_if = {
                                limit = {
                                    has_tech = nbc_nerve_weapon_4
                                }
                                set_variable = { Chemical_Damage = 0.45 }
                                set_variable = { ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.007 }
                                PREV = { add_province_modifier = { static_modifiers = { GV } days = 60 province = { all_provinces = yes } } }
                            }
                            else_if = {
                                limit = {
                                    has_tech = nbc_nerve_weapon_3
                                }
                                set_variable = { Chemical_Damage = 0.4 }
                                set_variable = { ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.006 }
                                PREV = { add_province_modifier = { static_modifiers = { VM } days = 60 province = { all_provinces = yes } } }
                            }
                            else_if = {
                                limit = {
                                    has_tech = nbc_nerve_weapon_2
                                }
                                set_variable = { Chemical_Damage = 0.35 }
                                set_variable = { ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.005 }
                                PREV = { add_province_modifier = { static_modifiers = { EA_3990 } days = 60 province = { all_provinces = yes } } }
                            }
                            else_if = {
                                limit = {
                                    has_tech = nbc_nerve_weapon_1
                                }
                                set_variable = { Chemical_Damage = 0.3 }
                                set_variable = { ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.004 }
                                PREV = { add_province_modifier = { static_modifiers = { vx } days = 60 province = { all_provinces = yes } } }
                            }
                            else_if = {
                                limit = {
                                    has_tech = nbc_nerve_weapon_0
                                }
                                set_variable = { Chemical_Damage = 0.25 }
                                set_variable = { ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.003 }
                                PREV = { add_province_modifier = { static_modifiers = { sarin } days = 60 province = { all_provinces = yes } } }
                            }
                            else = { }                        
                        }
                        if = {
                            limit = {
                                owner = {
                                    check_variable = {
                                        attacker_tag = THIS
                                    }
                                    check_variable = {
                                        attacker_state = PREV
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            damage_units = {
                                state = target_state
                                damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                ratio = yes
                            }
                            set_border_war_data = {
                                attacker = Target_State
                                defender = defender_state_test
                                defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                            }
                        }
                        else = {
                            set_temp_variable = {
                                defender_state_test = THIS
                            }
                            every_neighbor_state = {
                                limit = {
                                    set_temp_variable = {
                                        attacker_state_test = THIS
                                    }
                                    has_border_war_between = {
                                        attacker = attacker_state_test
                                        defender = defender_state_test
                                    }
                                }
                                set_temp_variable = {
                                    Target_State = THIS
                                }
                                damage_units = {
                                    state = target_state
                                    damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    ratio = yes
                                }
                                set_border_war_data = {
                                    attacker = Target_State
                                    defender = defender_state_test
                                    attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            civilian_casualties = state_population
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.002
                        }
                        multiply_variable = {
                            civilian_casualties = ROOT.Civilian_Modifier
                        }
                        add_manpower = civilian_casualties
                    }
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.05
                        ratio = yes
                    }
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
                    add_to_variable = {
                        global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 25
                        tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
                    }
                }
                visual_effect = {
                    entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }
            success = {

                                defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                            }
                        }

                            set_temp_variable = {

                            }

                }
            }

            var:target_state = {

                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS

                    reference = 100

                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id }
                }
            }

            var:target_state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }

        # Can prepare
        available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
        }
        launchable = {
        }

        target_type = {
            state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }
        custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
        target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon

        arrow = {
            type = air
        }
        #unit_model = {
        #    type = equipment
        #}

        unit_requirements = {
            equipment = {
                type = { tactical_bomber }
                amount = { min = 24 }
            }
        }

        starting_point = {
            types = { air_base }
        }

        success_factors = {
            success = {
                base = 0.65

                # Raiding unit modifiers
                experience = {
                    weight = 0.2
                    start_weight = -0.2
                    reference = 1.0
                }
                air_agility = {
                    reference = 100.0 # As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
                    weight = 0.1 # At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
                    start_weight = -0.1 # The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
                }
                reliability = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }
                air_defence = {
                    reference = 100
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.05
                }
                air_superiority = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.1
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }

                ### Target Modifiers
                anti_air = {
                    reference = 5
                    weight = -0.25
                }
                interception = {
                    reference = 500 #
                    weight = -0.2
                }
                intel = {
                    weight = 0.3
                    #start_reference = 50
                    reference = 100
                }
            }

            critical = {
                base = 0.15
            }
        }

        success_levels = {
            failure = {
                victim_effects = {
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.4
                        ratio = yes
                    }
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
                }
                visual_effect = {
                    entity = "raid_failed_entity"
                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }
            limited_success = {
                victim_effects = {
                    var:target_state = {
                        ROOT = {
                            set_variable = { Chemical_Damage = 0.1 }

                            PREV = { add_province_modifier = { static_modifiers = { white_phosphorus } days = 60 province = { all_provinces = yes } } }
                        }
                        if = {
                            limit = {
                                owner = {
                                    check_variable = {
                                        attacker_tag = THIS
                                    }
                                    check_variable = {
                                        attacker_state = PREV
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            damage_units = {
                                state = target_state
                                damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                ratio = yes
                            }
                            set_border_war_data = {
                                attacker = Target_State
                                defender = defender_state_test
                                defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                            }
                        }
                        else = {
                            set_temp_variable = {
                                defender_state_test = THIS
                            }
                            every_neighbor_state = {
                                limit = {
                                    set_temp_variable = {
                                        attacker_state_test = THIS
                                    }
                                    has_border_war_between = {
                                        attacker = attacker_state_test
                                        defender = defender_state_test
                                    }
                                }
                                set_temp_variable = {
                                    Target_State = THIS
                                }
                                damage_units = {
                                    state = target_state
                                    damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    ratio = yes
                                }
                                set_border_war_data = {
                                    attacker = Target_State
                                    defender = defender_state_test
                                    attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                }
                            }
                        }

                    }
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.05
                        ratio = yes
                    }
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
                    add_to_variable = {
                        global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 5
                        tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
                    }
                }
                visual_effect = {
                    entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }
            success = {
                victim_effects = {
                    var:target_state = {
                        ROOT = {
                            set_variable = { Chemical_Damage = 0.1 }

                            PREV = { add_province_modifier = { static_modifiers = { white_phosphorus } days = 60 province = { all_provinces = yes } } }

                                    defender = defender_state_test
                                    attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                }
                            }
                        }

                    }
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.2
                    add_to_variable = {
                        FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunistScore = 15
                        tooltip = Add_to_Communist_Victory_Points_TT

                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }
            limited_success = {
                victim_effects = {
                    var:target_state = {
                        ROOT = {
                            set_variable = { Chemical_Damage = 0.3 }
                            set_variable = { ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.001 }
                            PREV = { add_province_modifier = { static_modifiers = { napalm } days = 60 province = { all_provinces = yes } } }
                        }
                        if = {
                            limit = {
                                owner = {
                                    check_variable = {
                                        attacker_tag = THIS
                                    }
                                    check_variable = {
                                        attacker_state = PREV
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            damage_units = {
                                state = target_state
                                damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                ratio = yes
                            }
                            set_border_war_data = {
                                attacker = Target_State
                                defender = defender_state_test
                                defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                            }
                        }
                        else = {
                            set_temp_variable = {
                                defender_state_test = THIS
                            }
                            every_neighbor_state = {
                                limit = {
                                    set_temp_variable = {
                                        attacker_state_test = THIS
                                    }
                                    has_border_war_between = {
                                        attacker = attacker_state_test
                                        defender = defender_state_test
                                    }
                                }
                                set_temp_variable = {
                                    Target_State = THIS
                                }
                                damage_units = {
                                    state = target_state
                                    damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    ratio = yes
                                }
                                set_border_war_data = {
                                    attacker = Target_State
                                    defender = defender_state_test
                                    attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            civilian_casualties = state_population
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.002
                        }
                        multiply_variable = {
                            civilian_casualties = ROOT.Civilian_Modifier
                        }
                        add_manpower = civilian_casualties
                    }
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.05
                        ratio = yes
                    }
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
                    add_to_variable = {
                        global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 10
                        tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
                    }
                }
                visual_effect = {
                    entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }

    napalm_raid_commie = {
        days_to_prepare = 20

        ai_will_do = {
            base = 2
        }

        category = insurgency_raids

        command_power = 5

        allowed = {
        }

        # Raid Type is visible
        visible = {
            has_tech = NBC_research
            is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = ROOT.id }
            check_variable = {
                global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
            }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        # Specific Target is visible
        show_target = {
            OR = {
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id }
                }
            }

            var:target_state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }

        # Can prepare
        available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN
			country_exists = VIE
        }
        launchable = {
        }

            state = {
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }

                    reference = 100.0 # As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
                    weight = 0.1 # At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
                    start_weight = -0.1 # The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
                }
                reliability = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }
                air_defence = {
                    reference = 100
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.05
                }
                air_superiority = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.1
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }

                    reference = 5
                    weight = -0.25
                }

                    reference = 500 #
                    weight = -0.2
                }
                intel = {
                    weight = 0.3
                    #start_reference = 50
                    reference = 100
                }
            }

            critical = {
                base = 0.15
            }
        }

        success_levels = {

                            }
                            set_border_war_data = {
                                attacker = Target_State
                                defender = defender_state_test
                                defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                            }
                        }
                        else = {
                            set_temp_variable = {
                                defender_state_test = THIS
                            }
                            every_neighbor_state = {
                                limit = {
                                    set_temp_variable = {
                                        attacker_state_test = THIS
                                    }
                                    has_border_war_between = {
                                        attacker = attacker_state_test
                                        defender = defender_state_test
                                    }
                                }
                                set_temp_variable = {
                                    Target_State = THIS
                                }
                                damage_units = {
                                    state = target_state
                                    damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    org_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    str_damage = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                    ratio = yes
                                }
                                set_border_war_data = {
                                    attacker = Target_State
                                    defender = defender_state_test
                                    attacker_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            civilian_casualties = state_population
                        }
                        add_to_variable = {
                            ROOT.Civilian_Modifier = -0.002
                        }
                        multiply_variable = {
                            civilian_casualties = ROOT.Civilian_Modifier
                        }
                        add_manpower = civilian_casualties
                    }
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.05
                        ratio = yes
                    }
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
                    add_to_variable = {
                        global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 10
                        tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
                    }
                }
                visual_effect = {
                    entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }

    agent_orange_raid_anti_commie = {
        days_to_prepare = 20

        ai_will_do = {
            base = 2
        }

        category = insurgency_raids

        command_power = 5

        allowed = {
        }

        # Raid Type is visible
        visible = {
            has_tech = nbc_chemical_weapon_3
            OR = {
                is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProEthnic = ROOT.id }
                is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProFrance = ROOT.id }
                is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaProIndependence = ROOT.id }
            }
            check_variable = {
                global.Indochina_War_Active_Phase < 5
            }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
        }
        # Specific Target is visible
        show_target = {
            OR = {
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaCommunist = FROM.id }
                }
                FROM = {
                    is_in_array = { FRA.StruggleInvolvedNationsIndochinaKuomintang = FROM.id }
                }
            }

            var:target_state = {
                has_border_war = yes
                OR = {
                    is_core_of = VIN
                    is_core_of = CAM
                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }

        # Can prepare
        available = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			country_exists = VIN

                }
                reliability = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }
                air_defence = {
                    reference = 100
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.05

                air_superiority = {
                    reference = 1

                    start_weight = -0.1
                }

                ### Target Modifiers
                anti_air = {
                    reference = 5
                    weight = -0.25
                }
                interception = {
                    reference = 500 #
                    weight = -0.2
                }
                intel = {
                    weight = 0.3
                    #start_reference = 50
                    reference = 100
                }
            }

            critical = {
                base = 0.15
            }
        }
