                    is_core_of = LOS
                }
            }
        }
        custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_other_raid
        target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon

        arrow = {
            type = air
        }
        #unit_model = {
        #    type = equipment
        #}

        unit_requirements = {
            equipment = {
                type = { tactical_bomber }
                amount = { min = 24 }
            }
        }

        starting_point = {
            types = { air_base }
        }

        success_factors = {
            success = {
                base = 0.65

                # Raiding unit modifiers
                experience = {
                    weight = 0.2
                    start_weight = -0.2
                    reference = 1.0
                }
                air_agility = {
                    reference = 100.0 # As air unit agility approaches 100 (as defined by the value above), increase chance by this value
                    weight = 0.1 # At 0 agility, the modifier will have negative effect.
                    start_weight = -0.1 # The modifier will scale linearly, meaning at 50 agility, the modifier will be 0.
                }
                reliability = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }
                air_defence = {
                    reference = 100
                    weight = 0.05
                    start_weight = -0.05
                }
                air_superiority = {
                    reference = 1
                    weight = 0.1
                    start_weight = -0.1
                }

                ### Target Modifiers
                anti_air = {
                    reference = 5
                    weight = -0.25
                }
                interception = {
                    reference = 500 #
                    weight = -0.2
                }
                intel = {
                    weight = 0.3
                    #start_reference = 50
                    reference = 100
                }
            }

            critical = {
                base = 0.15
            }
        }

        success_levels = {
            failure = {
                victim_effects = {
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.4
                        ratio = yes
                    }
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.05
                }
                visual_effect = {
                    entity = "raid_failed_entity"
                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }
            limited_success = {
                victim_effects = {
                    var:target_state = {
                        ROOT = {
                            set_variable = { Chemical_Damage = 0.15 }

                            PREV = { add_province_modifier = { static_modifiers = { mustard_gas } days = 60 province = { all_provinces = yes } } }
                        }
                        if = {
                            limit = {
                                owner = {
                                    check_variable = {
                                        attacker_tag = THIS
                                    }
                                    check_variable = {
                                        attacker_state = PREV
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            set_border_war_data = {
                                attacker = Target_State
                                defender = defender_state_test
                                defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                            }
                        }
                        else = {
                            set_temp_variable = {
                                defender_state_test = THIS
                            }
                            every_neighbor_state = {
                                limit = {
                                    set_temp_variable = {
                                        attacker_state_test = THIS
                                    }
                                    has_border_war_between = {
                                        attacker = attacker_state_test
                                        defender = defender_state_test
                                    }
                                }
                                set_temp_variable = {
                                    Target_State = THIS
                                }
                                set_border_war_data = {
                                    attacker = Target_State
                                    defender = defender_state_test
                                    defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                }
                            }
                        }

                    }
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_damage_units = {
                        plane_loss = 0.05
                        ratio = yes
                    }
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.1
                    add_to_variable = {
                        global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 5
                        tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
                    }
                }
                visual_effect = {
                    entity = "air_raids_1_limited_success_entity"
                    animation = "attack"
                }
            }
            success = {
                victim_effects = {
                    var:target_state = {

                        if = {
                            limit = {
                                owner = {
                                    check_variable = {
                                        attacker_tag = THIS
                                    }
                                    check_variable = {
                                        attacker_state = PREV
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            set_border_war_data = {
                                attacker = Target_State
                                defender = defender_state_test
                                defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                            }
                        }
                        else = {
                            set_temp_variable = {
                                defender_state_test = THIS
                            }
                            every_neighbor_state = {
                                limit = {
                                    set_temp_variable = {
                                        attacker_state_test = THIS
                                    }
                                    has_border_war_between = {
                                        attacker = attacker_state_test
                                        defender = defender_state_test
                                    }
                                }
                                set_temp_variable = {
                                    Target_State = THIS
                                }
                                set_border_war_data = {
                                    attacker = Target_State
                                    defender = defender_state_test
                                    defender_modifier = ROOT.Chemical_Damage
                                }
                            }
                        }

                    }
                }
                actor_effects = {
                    raid_add_unit_experience = 0.35
                    add_to_variable = {
                        global.Indochina_War_Next_Phase_A_Points = 10
                        tooltip = Add_to_Escalation_Phase_TT
                    }
				}
				visual_effect = {
					entity = "air_raids_1_critical_success_entity"
					animation = "attack"
				}
			}
		}
	}

}
